\documentclass[10pt]{article}
\usepackage[a4paper,margin=2cm,landscape]{geometry}
\usepackage{amsmath,amssymb,longtable,array}
\setlength{\LTleft}{0pt}
\title{symembed: simple cases written out in full}
\date{\today}
\begin{document}
\maketitle

Each section shows one case. The slots of $w$ are listed with horizontal rules between $G$-orbits. Each group
element's action is shown as the row $g \cdot w$, which holds the coefficients of
$q_g(x) = \sum_j (g\cdot w)_j\, x^j$. Then every Bursarial output $E_{c,j}$, where
$\prod_g \bigl(y + q_g(x)\bigr) = \sum_c y^{|G| - c} \sum_j E_{c,j}\, x^j$, is shown expanded in the $d$'s and
$e$'s. The status column shows whether the safe reductions keep the output, and otherwise the exact identity used
to drop it. No determinants are omitted here, because no case contains parity. Expansions with more than
100 terms show only their term count. Every expansion appears in full in \texttt{simple\_cases\_full.txt}.
This file is generated by \texttt{symembed/explore/simple\_cases.py}; do not edit it by hand.

\section{$S(2)$ acting on $(a, b)$, $k = 2$}
$n = 2$, $k = 2$, $|G| = 2$, $m = 4$ slots. Bursarial outputs: 11; after safe reductions: 8 (exact linear dependencies checked for $c \le 2$). Quotient dimension (the minimum number of outputs, for generic inputs): 3.

\begin{longtable}{rcc}
slot $j$ & $w_j$ & degree in $L$ \\ \hline
0 & $d_{aa}$ & 2 \\
1 & $d_{bb}$ & 2 \\ \hline
2 & $d_{ab}$ & 2 \\ \hline
3 & $e_{ab}$ & 2 \\ \hline
\end{longtable}

\begin{longtable}{ll}
$g$ & $g \cdot w$ \\ \hline
$e$ & $(d_{aa},\ d_{bb},\ d_{ab},\ e_{ab})$ \\
$(a b)$ & $(d_{bb},\ d_{aa},\ d_{ab},\ -e_{ab})$ \\
\end{longtable}

\subsection*{$c = 1$}
\begin{longtable}{r>{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{0.62\textwidth}>{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{0.25\textwidth}}
output & expanded & safe reductions \\ \hline
$E_{1,0}$ & $= d_{aa} + d_{bb}$ & kept \\
$E_{1,1}$ & $= d_{aa} + d_{bb}$ & dropped: $= E_{1,0}$ \\
$E_{1,2}$ & $= 2 d_{ab}$ & kept \\
$E_{1,3}$ & $= 0$ & dropped: $= 0$ \\
\end{longtable}

\subsection*{$c = 2$}
\begin{longtable}{r>{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{0.62\textwidth}>{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{0.25\textwidth}}
output & expanded & safe reductions \\ \hline
$E_{2,0}$ & $= d_{aa} d_{bb}$ & kept \\
$E_{2,1}$ & $= d_{aa}^{2} + d_{bb}^{2}$ & kept \\
$E_{2,2}$ & $= d_{aa} d_{ab} + d_{aa} d_{bb} + d_{ab} d_{bb}$ & kept \\
$E_{2,3}$ & $= d_{aa} d_{ab} - d_{aa} e_{ab} + d_{ab} d_{bb} + d_{bb} e_{ab}$ & kept \\
$E_{2,4}$ & $= d_{aa} e_{ab} + d_{ab}^{2} - d_{bb} e_{ab}$ & kept \\
$E_{2,5}$ & $= 0$ & dropped: $= 0$ \\
$E_{2,6}$ & $= - e_{ab}^{2}$ & kept \\
\end{longtable}
